[2025-08-15 11:31:42,424][base_learner.py][line: 333][    INFO] [RANK0]: DI-engine DRL Policy
GraphModel(
  (_act): ReLU()
  (_q_network): DRQN(
    (encoder): FCEncoder(
      (act): ReLU()
      (init): Linear(in_features=30, out_features=256, bias=True)
      (main): Sequential(
        (0): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
        (1): ReLU()
      )
    )
    (rnn): GRU(256, 256)
    (head): DiscreteHead(
      (Q): Sequential(
        (0): Sequential(
          (0): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
          (1): ReLU()
        )
        (1): Sequential(
          (0): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=5, bias=True)
        )
      )
    )
  )
  (_region_encoder): Linear(in_features=16, out_features=10, bias=True)
  (Q): JointQ(
    (Qact): Sequential(
      (0): Linear(in_features=287, out_features=256, bias=True)
      (1): ReLU()
      (2): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
      (3): ReLU()
      (4): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=1, bias=True)
    )
    (Qst): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=1, bias=True)
  )
  (_transformer): Attention(
    (q): Sequential(
      (0): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=8, bias=True)
    )
    (k): Sequential(
      (0): Linear(in_features=8, out_features=8, bias=True)
    )
  )
  (_graph_encoder): GraphEncoder()
  (_graph_weight): GraphWeight(
    (q): GCN(
      (conv1): GCNConv(16, 8)
      (conv2): GCNConv(8, 8)
    )
    (k): GCN(
      (conv1): GCNConv(16, 8)
      (conv2): GCNConv(8, 8)
    )
  )
  (_neb_encoder): NeighborhoodEncoder(
    (encoder): GCN(
      (conv1): GCNConv(16, 8)
      (conv2): GCNConv(8, 8)
    )
  )
  (V): Sequential(
    (0): Linear(in_features=26, out_features=256, bias=True)
    (1): ReLU()
    (2): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=256, bias=True)
    (3): ReLU()
    (4): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=1, bias=True)
  )
  (action_encoding): Sequential(
    (0): Linear(in_features=261, out_features=261, bias=True)
    (1): ReLU()
    (2): Linear(in_features=261, out_features=261, bias=True)
  )
)
